# CorelDRAW feature inventory (parity reference for Stilus)

> **Provenance note (2026-09-26):** verbatim copy of the CorelDRAW Graphics Suite 2026 (v27.2) inventory compiled for the Stilus parity plan. The CD-### ids are stable and each is placed in exactly one row of [`../stilus-parity.md`](../stilus-parity.md). Do not renumber; a future CorelDRAW version appends new ids after CD-3041 (see [`../README.md`](../README.md)).

- **Product:** CorelDRAW Graphics Suite 2026 (CorelDRAW 2026), internal version 27.x, Windows.
- **Latest version as of 2026-09-26:** September 2026 Update, v27.2. The updates page dates it 2026-09-14 and the press release is dated 2026-09-15.
- **Release history:** March 2026 release v27.0.0.121, announced 2026-03-03. July 2026 Update v27.1, released 2026-07-14.
- **Compiled:** 2026-09-26.
- **Scope:** the CorelDRAW application (vector illustration and layout). Companion apps are listed briefly in the last section with category `companion`. Automation, cloud and AI rows are included and tagged with their category.

## Sources

- The main source is the CorelDRAW Graphics Suite 2025 (v26) Help, all 583 topics, at https://product.corel.com/help/CorelDRAW/540111186/Documentation-Windows/CorelDRAW-en/index.html. This is the newest Help that is publicly reachable (see the note at the end of this list).
- https://kb.corel.com/en/131071 (CorelDRAW 2026 User Guides index)
- https://www.coreldraw.com/en/support/updates/
- https://www.coreldraw.com/en/support/updates/cdgs2026/update.html
- https://www.coreldraw.com/en/learn/tutorials/new-in-march-2026/
- https://www.coreldraw.com/en/blog/product/coreldraw-graphics-suite-2026/
- https://www.corel.com/en/newsroom/news/press-releases/20260303-introducing-ai-powered-creativity-coreldraw-graphics-suite/
- https://www.globenewswire.com/news-release/2026/09/15/3362194/0/en/ai-vector-graphics-tools-power-creative-workflows-in-coreldraw-graphics-suite-2026.html
- https://signsofthetimes.com/coreldraw-graphics-suite-september-2026-update-available/
- https://www.coreldraw.com/en/product/coreldraw/
- https://www.coreldraw.com/en/blog/product/coreldraw-graphics-suite-2025/

**Note on the Help source:** no public 2026 (v27) online Help could be found. The v27 Help and User Guide links in the Knowledge Base go through apps.corel.com, which refused connections, and the versioned product.corel.com paths after 540111186 return 404. So v27 additions come from Corel's what's-new pages, release pages and press releases, and every other row cites the 2025 Help.

**Caveats:**
- Some menu items are never named in a Help procedure (some View modes, the Order and Align submenu keys, some Text menu items). These were filled in from the v25/v26 menu layout, and their placement may differ slightly after the 2026 UI refresh.
- Keyboard shortcuts are the long-standing defaults. Only some of them are confirmed in the Help text.

## Legend

- **Kind:** tool, command, docker, docker-option, effect, dialog, format, preference, behavior.
- **Category:** core, format, print, automation, cloud, ai, companion.
- **IDs:** CD-### numbers are sequential and unique across the whole file.
- **Row granularity:** options that change behavior (each mode, type or setting) get their own row.
